# MONITOR FEED -- phase-by-phase record for the chat monitor (Claude) + Logan

GOAL: Prism editor to TRUE production. BAR: photoreal/premium 4K motion-graphics; acceptable==FAIL; constrained preview-pane + desktop matter most; mobile fast. VERIFICATION: computer-use advocate (opus 4.8 1M) in-loop + numeric harness + chat-monitor (Claude) end review. HANDS-OFF: sentinels (unlimited auto-resume) + chain.

HOW THIS WORKS: build agents append phase events here at each boundary / verdict / MUST-FIX / completion; the chat monitor appends assessments + routing decisions on each check. APPEND-ONLY -- never delete prior lines. The monitor reads this (plus git log + ledgers + frames) on every check to reconstruct the run and pick the best route, knowing the goal.

== APP REALITY (DONE, 9/9 phases, advocate 0 MUST-FIX) ==
Monitor (Claude) caught 2 real defects the advocate passed: (1) oval/stipple/flat-corner surface on landscape viewports, (2) Arrival hero absent on desktop/tablet. Routed -> PROD-FINISH fix-pass.

== PROD-FINISH fix-pass (RUNNING) ==
BASE done: harness + numeric screen-rect hook; both defects confirmed by number (atmosphere 0/20, heroes 17/20).
Phase A DONE: full-viewport premium nebula atmosphere; oval/stipple/flat-corners killed; atmosphere 20/20.
Phase B DONE: hero preload + punchy framing; Arrival watch present + lit on desktop/tablet.
Phase C IN PROGRESS: production functional validation + capstone advocate. On completion marker -> chain auto-fires Node Editor.
MONITOR ROUTE (planned): on completion, do the live drive-through (real browser: all hubs, device modes, function->overlay, corners + hero at DPR-2, real-GPU perf trace) before accepting; micro-fix anything the advocate missed (as on App Reality); then let Node Editor proceed (or pause the chain to validate first).

== QUEUE-PREP (RUNNING, parallel, conflict-free) ==
Drafting 3D-Background-Library + Guided-Tips prompts with current-June-2026 research. Markdown-only; no code/browser/git.

== NODE EDITOR (QUEUED -- auto-fires after fix-pass via chain) ==
Will self-report phase events below.
